- **README**: `bin/crew.js` calls `require('../lib/core/cli').main(argv)`. For `crew &lt;group&gt; ...` it loads `lib/commands/&lt;group&gt;.js` on demand, so adding a group never touches the dispatcher. Files starting with `_` and non-`.js` files are ignored. A module that throws on load only hides itself. ([ynyrworks/crewgate](https://github.com/ynyrworks/crewgate/blob/HEAD/plugins/crewgate/lib/commands/README.md) · ★3)
- **README**: Rhai scripts implementing player and builder commands. Each file exports: ([zombieCraig/IronMUD-public](https://github.com/zombieCraig/IronMUD-public/blob/HEAD/scripts/commands/README.md) · ★3)

- **README**: This directory exists for **user-custom** slash commands. All MAP slash commands now ship as Skills (`.claude/skills/map-*/SKILL.md`) which give the same `/map-*` interface but with progressive disclosure (skill body loads on demand instead of always living in context). ([azalio/cpu-idle-operator](https://github.com/azalio/cpu-idle-operator/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/README.md) · ★2)
- **README**: This directory owns private market mutation operations and command boundaries: ([c4rl0s04/biwenger-euroleague-stats](https://github.com/c4rl0s04/biwenger-euroleague-stats/blob/HEAD/src/features/market/commands/README.md) · ★2)
- **README**: Claude Code's TUI maps every `.md` file under a plugin's `commands/` directory to a slash command. The filename, minus the `.md` extension, is the slash invocation: `commands/reflect.md` becomes `/reflect`. The frontmatter declares the command's metadata (description, argument hint, allowed-tools scope); the body becomes the prompt Claude reads when the user runs the command. ([calisweetleaf/Marketplace-for-AI-Agentic-Operators](https://github.com/calisweetleaf/Marketplace-for-AI-Agentic-Operators/blob/HEAD/Plugins/Mentat/commands/README.md) · ★2)
- **README**: 这些文件是预设流程的正文，每条流程只维护一份。先初始化，带着一件自己的事用起来。日常直接告诉 AI「帮我记下来」「整理一下」「这件事做完了」「找找以前的经验」即可，不用先记命令。 ([charlespai/life-compounding-system](https://github.com/charlespai/life-compounding-system/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/README.md) · ★2)
- **README**: This directory contains custom slash commands for Claude Code to assist with MIDI Markdown development. ([cjgdev/midi-markdown](https://github.com/cjgdev/midi-markdown/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/README.md) · ★2)

- **README**: Ready-to-use slash command files for Claude Code. Each file is a drop-in `.md` file you copy to your project's `.claude/commands/` directory. ([CodeWithBehnam/cc-docs](https://github.com/CodeWithBehnam/cc-docs/blob/HEAD/commands/README.md) · ★2)

- **README**: Slash commands for this repository. Each is a thin pointer at the matching file in [`.github/prompts/`](../../.github/prompts/), which is where the actual procedure lives — so there is one copy to keep current, and Copilot, Cursor and Claude all read the same source. ([Danathar/zfs-aurora-complex](https://github.com/Danathar/zfs-aurora-complex/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/README.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)

- **README**: A **command** is a Markdown file with YAML frontmatter that becomes a slash command in Claude Code (and compatible agents). It's the lightest-weight component in this toolkit — useful for a short, repeatable instruction that doesn't need the structure of a full skill (playbooks, references, templates). ([DiUS/agent-toolkit](https://github.com/DiUS/agent-toolkit/blob/HEAD/commands/README.md) · ★2)
